Global interpretation:
  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.

  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.

Global interpretation:
  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

Global interpretation:
  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

Global interpretation:
  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

Global interpretation:
  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

Global interpretation:
  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

Global interpretation:
  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

Global interpretation:
  - If all models improve strongly when lowering act_thresh, the main issue is SED calibration.
  - If pred/tgt remains low even at very low thresholds, activity probabilities are systematically under-confident.
  - If pred/tgt is reasonable but LE_CD remains high, localization / DOA tracking is the dominant problem.
  - If sed_eval SED metrics improve but SELD metrics remain poor, localization is likely the bottleneck.
  - If internal metrics and sed_eval disagree strongly, the internal metric implementation may not be fully aligned with DCASE protocol.

  - If predicted DOA norms are close to zero on target-active slots, suspect DOA head collapse or loss imbalance.
  - If a coordinate transform strongly improves angular error, suspect coordinate convention or channel/axis mismatch.

Global interpretation rules:
  - If oracle DOA is poor even when target activity is known, the problem is not only SED.
  - If predicted DOA norms are close to zero on target-active slots, suspect DOA head collapse or loss imbalance.
  - If a coordinate transform strongly improves angular error, suspect coordinate convention or channel/axis mismatch.
  - If static sources are localised well but moving sources are not, suspect trajectory tracking limitations.
  - If predicted angular velocity >> target velocity, suspect jittery localisation.
  - If predicted angular velocity << target velocity on moving sources, suspect frozen DOA estimates.

Global interpretation rules:
  - If oracle DOA is poor even when target activity is known, the problem is not only SED.
  - If predicted DOA norms are close to zero on target-active slots, suspect DOA head collapse or loss imbalance.
  - If a coordinate transform strongly improves angular error, suspect coordinate convention or channel/axis mismatch.
  - If static sources are localised well but moving sources are not, suspect trajectory tracking limitations.
  - If predicted angular velocity >> target velocity, suspect jittery localisation.
  - If predicted angular velocity << target velocity on moving sources, suspect frozen DOA estimates.

Global interpretation rules:
  - If oracle DOA is poor even when target activity is known, the problem is not only SED.
  - If predicted DOA norms are close to zero on target-active slots, suspect DOA head collapse or loss imbalance.
  - If a coordinate transform strongly improves angular error, suspect coordinate convention or channel/axis mismatch.
  - If static sources are localised well but moving sources are not, suspect trajectory tracking limitations.
  - If predicted angular velocity >> target velocity, suspect jittery localisation.
  - If predicted angular velocity << target velocity on moving sources, suspect frozen DOA estimates.

Global interpretation rules:
  - If oracle DOA is poor even when target activity is known, the problem is not only SED.
  - If predicted DOA norms are close to zero on target-active slots, suspect DOA head collapse or loss imbalance.
  - If a coordinate transform strongly improves angular error, suspect coordinate convention or channel/axis mismatch.
  - If static sources are localised well but moving sources are not, suspect trajectory tracking limitations.
  - If predicted angular velocity >> target velocity, suspect jittery localisation.
  - If predicted angular velocity << target velocity on moving sources, suspect frozen DOA estimates.

Global interpretation rules:
  - If oracle DOA is poor even when target activity is known, the problem is not only SED.
  - If predicted DOA norms are close to zero on target-active slots, suspect DOA head collapse or loss imbalance.
  - If a coordinate transform strongly improves angular error, suspect coordinate convention or channel/axis mismatch.
  - If static sources are localised well but moving sources are not, suspect trajectory tracking limitations.
  - If predicted angular velocity >> target velocity, suspect jittery localisation.
  - If predicted angular velocity << target velocity on moving sources, suspect frozen DOA estimates.
